daily_report_screen version 2
import 'package:cloud_firestore/cloud_firestore.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:fl_chart/fl_chart.dart';
import 'package:provider/provider.dart';
import 'package:syncfusion_flutter_charts/charts.dart';
import 'package:intl/intl.dart';
import 'sales_data.dart';

class DailyReportScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  final DateTime date;
  final SalesData salesData;
  final bool showAllDays;

  const DailyReportScreen({
    super.key,
    required this.date,
    required this.salesData,
    this.showAllDays = false,
  });

  @override
  State<DailyReportScreen> createState() => _DailyReportScreenState();
}

final NumberFormat _currencyFormat = NumberFormat.currency(
  locale: 'en_PH',
  symbol: '₱',
  decimalDigits: 2,
);

extension ProductMapExtensions on Map<String, dynamic> {
  String get name => this['name']?.toString() ?? 'Unknown';
  double get price => (this['price'] as num?)?.toDouble() ?? 0.0;
}

enum SortBy { date, name, price }

class _DailyReportScreenState extends State<DailyReportScreen> {
  bool _showForecast = false;
  late List<SalesDataPoint> _forecastData;
  final SortBy _sortBy = SortBy.date;
  final bool _sortAscending = true;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _forecastData = _generateForecastData(widget.salesData);
  }
  

  DateTime _parseFirestoreDate(dynamic date) {
    try {
      if (date == null) return DateTime.now();
      if (date is Timestamp) return date.toDate();
      if (date is DateTime) return date;
      if (date is String) return DateTime.parse(date);
    } catch (e, stack) {
      debugPrint('Firestore error: $e\n$stack');
      return DateTime.now();
    }
    debugPrint('Unsupported date type: ${date.runtimeType}');
    return DateTime.now();
  }

  List<SalesDataPoint> _generateForecastData(SalesData salesData) {
    final weekdays = ['Mon', 'Tue', 'Wed', 'Thu', 'Fri', 'Sat', 'Sun'];
    final weeklyAverages = List<double>.filled(7, 0.0);
    final dayCounts = List<int>.filled(7, 0);

    for (var dayProducts in salesData.currentWeekData.values) {
      for (var product in dayProducts) {
        final date = _parseFirestoreDate(product['date']);
        final weekday = date.weekday - 1;
        weeklyAverages[weekday] += (product['price'] as num).toDouble();
        dayCounts[weekday]++;
      }
    }

    return List.generate(7, (index) {
      final avg = dayCounts[index] > 0
          ? weeklyAverages[index] / dayCounts[index]
          : 500.0;
      final forecastValue = avg * 1.1;
      return SalesDataPoint(weekdays[index], forecastValue);
    });
  }

  List<PieChartSectionData> _buildPieChartData(
      List<Map<String, dynamic>> products, double total) {
    if (products.isEmpty || total <= 0) {
      return [
        PieChartSectionData(
          color: Colors.grey[300],
          value: 1,
          title: 'No Data',
          radius: 20,
          titleStyle: const TextStyle(
            fontSize: 14,
            fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
            color: Colors.black54,
          ),
        )
      ];
    }

    // Group products by name and calculate totals
    final productTotals = <String, double>{};
    for (var product in products) {
      final name = product['name'] ?? 'Unknown';
      final price = (product['price'] as num).toDouble();
      productTotals.update(name, (value) => value + price,
          ifAbsent: () => price);
    }

    // Sort by value descending
    final sortedEntries = productTotals.entries.toList()
      ..sort((a, b) => b.value.compareTo(a.value));

    // Take top 5 and group the rest as "Others"
    final topProducts = sortedEntries.take(5).toList();
    final othersTotal = sortedEntries
        .skip(5)
        .fold(0.0, (runningTotal, entry) => runningTotal + entry.value);

    // Use a vibrant color palette
    final colors = [
      Colors.indigo.shade400,
      Colors.teal.shade400,
      Colors.amber.shade600,
      Colors.pink.shade400,
      Colors.deepPurple.shade400,
      Colors.grey.shade500,
    ];

    final sections = <PieChartSectionData>[];

    // Build sections for top products
    for (int i = 0; i < topProducts.length; i++) {
      final entry = topProducts[i];
      final percentage = (entry.value / total * 100).toStringAsFixed(1);

      sections.add(
        PieChartSectionData(
          color: colors[i],
          value: entry.value,
          title: double.parse(percentage) > 5
              ? '$percentage%'
              : '', // Only show if significant
          radius: 24,
          titleStyle: TextStyle(
            fontSize: 12,
            fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
            color: Colors.white,
            shadows: const [Shadow(color: Colors.black54, blurRadius: 3)],
          ),
          borderSide: BorderSide(
            color: Color.fromRGBO(255, 255, 255, 0.5),
            width: 2,
          ),
          badgeWidget: _buildBadge(entry.key, entry.value),
          badgePositionPercentageOffset: 1.2,
        ),
      );
    }

    // Add "Others" section if needed
    if (othersTotal > 0) {
      final othersPercentage = (othersTotal / total * 100).toStringAsFixed(1);
      final showPercentage = double.parse(othersPercentage) > 5;

      sections.add(
        PieChartSectionData(
          color: colors.last,
          value: othersTotal,
          title: showPercentage ? '$othersPercentage%' : '',
          radius: 24,
          titleStyle: TextStyle(
            fontSize: 12,
            fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
            color: Colors.white,
            shadows: const [Shadow(color: Colors.black54, blurRadius: 3)],
          ),
          borderSide: BorderSide(
            color: Color.fromRGBO(255, 255, 255, 0.5),
            width: 2,
          ),
          badgeWidget: _buildBadge('Others', othersTotal),
          badgePositionPercentageOffset: 1.2,
        ),
      );
    }

    return sections;
  }

  Widget _buildBadge(String label, double value) {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8, vertical: 4),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Colors.white,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
        boxShadow: [
          BoxShadow(
            color: Colors.black12,
            blurRadius: 4,
          ),
        ],
      ),
      child: Column(
        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
        children: [
          Text(
            label,
            style: TextStyle(
              fontSize: 10,
              fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
              color: Colors.grey.shade800,
            ),
          ),
          Text(
            _currencyFormat.format(value),
            style: TextStyle(
              fontSize: 12,
              fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
              color: Theme.of(context).primaryColor,
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildSummaryCard(double total, int productCount) {
    return Card(
      elevation: 4,
      child: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
        child: Column(
          children: [
            Text(
              widget.showAllDays ? 'Weekly Summary' : 'Daily Summary',
              style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 20, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 12),
            Row(
              mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
              children: [
                const Text('Total Sales:',
                    style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                Text(
                  _currencyFormat.format(total),
                  style: TextStyle(
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                    fontSize: 16,
                    color: Theme.of(context).primaryColor,
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 8),
            Row(
              mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
              children: [
                const Text('Products Sold:',
                    style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                Text(
                  '$productCount',
                  style: TextStyle(
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                    fontSize: 16,
                    color: Theme.of(context).primaryColor,
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
            if (widget.showAllDays) ...[
              const SizedBox(height: 8),
              Row(
                mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                children: [
                  const Text('Average Daily:',
                      style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                  Text(
                    '₱${(total / 7).toStringAsFixed(2)}',
                    style: TextStyle(
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                      fontSize: 16,
                      color: Theme.of(context).primaryColor,
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ],
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  String _formatDate(DateTime date) {
    return DateFormat('EEE, MMM d, y').format(date);
  }

  String _formatTime(DateTime time) {
    return DateFormat('h:mm a').format(time);
  }

  Widget _buildPieChartSection(
      List<PieChartSectionData> sections, double total) {
    return Column(
      children: [
        SizedBox(
          height: MediaQuery.of(context).size.height * 0.4,
          child: Stack(
            alignment: Alignment.center,
            children: [
              PieChart(
                PieChartData(
                  sections: sections,
                  centerSpaceRadius: 80,
                  sectionsSpace: 0,
                  startDegreeOffset: -90,
                  pieTouchData: PieTouchData(
                    enabled: true,
                    touchCallback: (FlTouchEvent event, pieTouchResponse) {
                      setState(() {
                        if (event is FlTapUpEvent && pieTouchResponse != null) {
                          // Handle tap on sections if needed
                        }
                      });
                    },
                  ),
                ),
                duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 500),
                curve: Curves.easeInOut,
              ),
              Column(
                mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                children: [
                  Text(
                    'TOTAL SALES',
                    style: TextStyle(
                      fontSize: 14,
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                      color: Colors.grey.shade600,
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 4),
                  Text(
                    _currencyFormat.format(total),
                    style: TextStyle(
                      fontSize: 24,
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                      color: Theme.of(context).primaryColor,
                    ),
                  ),
                  if (widget.showAllDays) ...[
                    const SizedBox(height: 4),
                    Text(
                      '${sections.length} ${sections.length == 1 ? 'Category' : 'Categories'}',
                      style: TextStyle(
                        fontSize: 12,
                        color: Colors.grey.shade600,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ],
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 8),
        _buildLegend(sections),
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _buildLegend(List<PieChartSectionData> sections) {
    return Wrap(
      alignment: WrapAlignment.center,
      spacing: 12,
      runSpacing: 8,
      children: sections.map((section) {
        return Row(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          children: [
            Container(
              width: 16,
              height: 16,
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: section.color,
                shape: BoxShape.circle,
                border: Border.all(
                  color: Colors.white,
                  width: 2,
                ),
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(width: 6),
            Text(
              section.title.isNotEmpty
                  ? section.title.split('\n').first.replaceAll('%', '')
                  : 'Others',
              style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 12),
            ),
          ],
        );
      }).toList(),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildForecastSection() {
    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      children: [
        const Text(
          'Sales Forecast (Next 7 Days)',
          style: TextStyle(fontSize: 18, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 8),
        SizedBox(
          height: 250,
          child: SfCartesianChart(
            primaryXAxis: CategoryAxis(),
            primaryYAxis: NumericAxis(
              title: AxisTitle(text: 'Sales Amount (₱)'),
            ),
            series: <CartesianSeries>[
              LineSeries<SalesDataPoint, String>(
                dataSource: _forecastData,
                xValueMapper: (SalesDataPoint sales, _) => sales.day,
                yValueMapper: (SalesDataPoint sales, _) => sales.amount,
                dataLabelSettings: DataLabelSettings(
                  isVisible: true,
                  labelAlignment: ChartDataLabelAlignment.top,
                  textStyle: TextStyle(fontSize: 10),
                  builder: (dynamic data, dynamic point, dynamic series,
                      int pointIndex, int seriesIndex) {
                    return Text(_currencyFormat.format(data.amount));
                  },
                ),
                name: 'Forecasted Sales',
                color: Theme.of(context).primaryColor,
              )
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  List<Map<String, dynamic>> _getSortedProducts(
      List<Map<String, dynamic>> products) {
    return List.from(products)
      ..sort((a, b) {
        final dateA = _parseFirestoreDate(a['date']);
        final dateB = _parseFirestoreDate(b['date']);

        switch (_sortBy) {
          case SortBy.name:
            return _sortAscending
                ? a.name.compareTo(b.name)
                : b.name.compareTo(a.name);
          case SortBy.price:
            return _sortAscending
                ? a.price.compareTo(b.price)
                : b.price.compareTo(a.price);
          case SortBy.date:
            return _sortAscending
                ? dateA.compareTo(dateB)
                : dateB.compareTo(dateA);
        }
      });
  }

  Map<String, List<Map<String, dynamic>>> _groupProductsByDay(
      List<Map<String, dynamic>> products) {
    final Map<String, List<Map<String, dynamic>>> grouped = {};

    for (var product in products) {
      if (product['date'] != null) {
        final date = _parseFirestoreDate(product['date']);
        final day = DateFormat('EEEE, MMM d').format(date);

        if (!grouped.containsKey(day)) {
          grouped[day] = [];
        }
        grouped[day]!.add(product);
      }
    }

    return grouped;
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final salesData = Provider.of<SalesData>(context);
    List<Map<String, dynamic>> productsToShow = [];
    double totalAmount = 0;

    if (widget.showAllDays) {
      for (var dayProducts in salesData.currentWeekData.values) {
        productsToShow.addAll(dayProducts);
      }
      totalAmount = productsToShow.fold(0.0, (runningTotal, product) {
        return runningTotal + (product['price'] as num).toDouble();
      });
    } else {
      final dayIndex = widget.date.weekday % 7;
      productsToShow = salesData.currentWeekData[dayIndex] ?? [];
      totalAmount = salesData.getDailyTotal(dayIndex);
    }

    if (salesData.isLoading) {
      return const Scaffold(
        body: Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator()),
      );
    }

    List<PieChartSectionData> pieChartSections =
        _buildPieChartData(productsToShow, totalAmount);

    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: Text(widget.showAllDays
            ? 'Weekly Products Report'
            : 'Daily Report - ${_formatDate(widget.date)}'),
        actions: [
          IconButton(
            icon: Icon(_showForecast ? Icons.insert_chart : Icons.trending_up),
            onPressed: () {
              setState(() {
                _showForecast = !_showForecast;
              });
            },
            tooltip: _showForecast ? 'Show Current Data' : 'Show Forecast',
          ),
          if (widget.showAllDays)
            PopupMenuButton<String>(
              onSelected: (value) {},
              itemBuilder: (context) => [
                const PopupMenuItem(
                  value: 'date',
                  child: Text('Sort by Date'),
                ),
                const PopupMenuItem(
                  value: 'name',
                  child: Text('Sort by Name'),
                ),
                const PopupMenuItem(
                  value: 'price',
                  child: Text('Sort by Price'),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          IconButton(
            icon: const Icon(Icons.refresh),
            onPressed: () {
              setState(() {
                _forecastData = _generateForecastData(widget.salesData);
              });
            },
            tooltip: 'Refresh Forecast',
          ),
        ],
      ),
      body: SingleChildScrollView(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            _buildSummaryCard(totalAmount, productsToShow.length),
            const SizedBox(height: 20),
            if (_showForecast) ...[
              _buildForecastSection(),
              const SizedBox(height: 20),
            ] else ...[
              _buildPieChartSection(pieChartSections, totalAmount),
              const SizedBox(height: 20),
            ],
            Text(
              widget.showAllDays
                  ? 'All Products Sold This Week:'
                  : 'Products Sold:',
              style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 18, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 8),
            productsToShow.isEmpty
                ? const Center(child: Text('No products sold'))
                : widget.showAllDays
                    ? Column(
                        children: _groupProductsByDay(productsToShow)
                            .entries
                            .map((entry) {
                          final dayTotal = entry.value.fold(
                              0.0,
                              (runningTotal, p) =>
                                  runningTotal +
                                  (p['price'] as num).toDouble());

                          return Column(
                            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                            children: [
                              Padding(
                                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                                    vertical: 8.0, horizontal: 16.0),
                                child: Row(
                                  mainAxisAlignment:
                                      MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                                  children: [
                                    Text(
                                      entry.key,
                                      style: const TextStyle(
                                          fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                                          fontSize: 16),
                                    ),
                                    Text(
                                      '₱${dayTotal.toStringAsFixed(2)}',
                                      style: const TextStyle(
                                          fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                                    ),
                                  ],
                                ),
                              ),
                              ...entry.value.map((product) {
                                final productDate =
                                    _parseFirestoreDate(product['date']);
                                final productName =
                                    product['name']?.toString() ?? 'Unknown';
                                final productPrice =
                                    (product['price'] as num?)?.toDouble() ??
                                        0.0;

                                return Card(
                                  margin: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                                      vertical: 4, horizontal: 8),
                                  child: ListTile(
                                    title: Text(productName),
                                    subtitle: Text(_formatTime(productDate)),
                                    trailing: Text(
                                      _currencyFormat.format(productPrice),
                                      style: TextStyle(
                                          fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                                    ),
                                  ),
                                );
                              }),
                              const Divider(),
                            ],
                          );
                        }).toList(),
                      )
                    : Column(
                        children:
                            _getSortedProducts(productsToShow).map((product) {
                          final productDate =
                              _parseFirestoreDate(product['date']);
                          return Card(
                            margin: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 4),
                            child: ListTile(
                              title: Text(product['name'] ?? 'Unknown'),
                              subtitle: Text(_formatTime(productDate)),
                              trailing: Text(
                                _currencyFormat.format(product['price']),
                                style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
                              ),
                            ),
                          );
                        }).toList(),
                      ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}


class SalesDataPoint {
  final String day;
  final double amount;

  SalesDataPoint(this.day, this.amount);
}

///////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////

import 'package:cloud_firestore/cloud_firestore.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:fl_chart/fl_chart.dart';
import 'package:provider/provider.dart';
import 'package:syncfusion_flutter_charts/charts.dart';
import 'package:intl/intl.dart';
import 'sales_data.dart';

class DailyReportScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  final DateTime date;
  final SalesData salesData;
  final bool showAllDays;

  const DailyReportScreen({
    super.key,
    required this.date,
    required this.salesData,
    this.showAllDays = false,
  });

  @override
  State<DailyReportScreen> createState() => _DailyReportScreenState();
}

final NumberFormat _currencyFormat = NumberFormat.currency(
  locale: 'en_PH',
  symbol: '₱',
  decimalDigits: 2,
);

class _DailyReportScreenState extends State<DailyReportScreen> {
  bool _showForecast = false;
  late List<SalesDataPoint> _forecastData;
  late List<SalesDataPoint> _adjustedForecast;

  final Map<String, bool> _externalFactors = {
    'Bad Weather': false,
    'Local Event': false,
    'Promotion': false,
  };

  final Map<String, double> _factorImpacts = {
    'Bad Weather': -0.15,
    'Local Event': 0.30,
    'Promotion': 0.25,
  };

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _forecastData = _generateForecastData(widget.salesData);
    _adjustedForecast = List.from(_forecastData);
  }

  List<SalesDataPoint> _generateForecastData(SalesData salesData) {
    final weekdays = ['Mon', 'Tue', 'Wed', 'Thu', 'Fri', 'Sat', 'Sun'];
    final weeklyAverages = List<double>.filled(7, 0.0);
    final dayCounts = List<int>.filled(7, 0);

    for (var dayProducts in salesData.currentWeekData.values) {
      for (var product in dayProducts) {
        final date = _parseFirestoreDate(product['date']);
        final weekday = date.weekday - 1;
        weeklyAverages[weekday] += (product['price'] as num).toDouble();
        dayCounts[weekday]++;
      }
    }

    return List.generate(7, (index) {
      final forecastValue = dayCounts[index] > 0
          ? _calculateAdjustedForecast(weeklyAverages[index], dayCounts[index])
          : 0.0;
      return SalesDataPoint(weekdays[index], forecastValue);
    });
  }

  double _calculateAdjustedForecast(double total, int count) {
    final average = total / count;
    // Reduce impact of items over 10,000 pesos
    if (average > 10000) {
      return average * 1.05; // Only 5% increase for high-value items
    }
    return average * 1.1; // Normal 10% increase
  }

  DateTime _parseFirestoreDate(dynamic date) {
    try {
      if (date == null) return DateTime.now();
      if (date is Timestamp) return date.toDate();
      if (date is DateTime) return date;
      if (date is String) return DateTime.parse(date);
    } catch (e, stack) {
      debugPrint('Firestore error: $e\n$stack');
      return DateTime.now();
    }
    debugPrint('Unsupported date type: ${date.runtimeType}');
    return DateTime.now();
  }

  List<SalesDataPoint> _applyExternalFactors(List<SalesDataPoint> forecast) {
    if (!_externalFactors.containsValue(true)) return forecast;

    return forecast.map((point) {
      double adjustedAmount = point.amount;

      _externalFactors.forEach((factor, isActive) {
        if (isActive) {
          adjustedAmount *= (1 + _factorImpacts[factor]!);
        }
      });

      return SalesDataPoint(point.day, adjustedAmount);
    }).toList();
  }

  Widget _buildExternalFactorsPanel() {
    return Card(
      elevation: 2,
      margin: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 8),
      child: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(12),
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            const Text('External Factors',
                style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 16)),
            const Divider(),
            ..._externalFactors.keys
                .map((factor) => _buildFactorSwitch(factor)),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildFactorSwitch(String factor) {
    return SwitchListTile(
      title: Row(
        children: [
          Text(_getFactorEmoji(factor)),
          const SizedBox(width: 8),
          Text(factor),
          const SizedBox(width: 4),
          IconButton(
            icon: const Icon(Icons.info_outline, size: 16),
            onPressed: () => _showFactorExplanation(context, factor),
          ),
        ],
      ),
      subtitle: Text(
        "${(_factorImpacts[factor]! * 100).toInt()}% impact",
        style: TextStyle(
          color: _factorImpacts[factor]! > 0 ? Colors.green : Colors.red,
        ),
      ),
      value: _externalFactors[factor]!,
      onChanged: (value) {
        setState(() {
          _externalFactors[factor] = value;
          _adjustedForecast = _applyExternalFactors(_forecastData);
        });
      },
    );
  }

  String _getFactorEmoji(String factor) {
    switch (factor) {
      case 'Bad Weather':
        return '☔';
      case 'Local Event':
        return '🎉';
      case 'Promotion':
        return '📢';
      default:
        return '';
    }
  }

  void _showFactorExplanation(BuildContext context, String factor) {
    final explanations = {
      'Bad Weather':
          'Reduces forecast by 15% due to expected lower foot traffic',
      'Local Event':
          'Increases forecast by 30% for special events in your area',
      'Promotion':
          'Boosts forecast by 25% when running active marketing campaigns',
    };

    showDialog(
      context: context,
      builder: (context) => AlertDialog(
        title: Text('$factor Impact'),
        content: Text(explanations[factor]!),
        actions: [
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context),
            child: const Text('OK'),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  List<PieChartSectionData> _buildPieChartData(
      List<Map<String, dynamic>> products, double total) {
    if (products.isEmpty || total <= 0) {
      return [
        PieChartSectionData(
          color: Colors.grey[300],
          value: 1,
          title: 'No Data',
          radius: 20,
          titleStyle: const TextStyle(
            fontSize: 14,
            fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
            color: Colors.black54,
          ),
        )
      ];
    }

    final productTotals = <String, double>{};
    for (var product in products) {
      final name = product['name'] ?? 'Unknown';
      final price = (product['price'] as num).toDouble();
      productTotals.update(name, (value) => value + price,
          ifAbsent: () => price);
    }

    final sortedEntries = productTotals.entries.toList()
      ..sort((a, b) => b.value.compareTo(a.value));

    final topProducts = sortedEntries.take(5).toList();
    final othersTotal = sortedEntries
        .skip(5)
        .fold(0.0, (runningTotal, entry) => runningTotal + entry.value);

    final colors = [
      Colors.indigo.shade400,
      Colors.teal.shade400,
      Colors.amber.shade600,
      Colors.pink.shade400,
      Colors.deepPurple.shade400,
      Colors.grey.shade500,
    ];

    final sections = <PieChartSectionData>[];

    for (int i = 0; i < topProducts.length; i++) {
      final entry = topProducts[i];
      final percentage = (entry.value / total * 100).toStringAsFixed(1);

      sections.add(
        PieChartSectionData(
          color: colors[i],
          value: entry.value,
          title: double.parse(percentage) > 5 ? '$percentage%' : '',
          radius: 24,
          titleStyle: const TextStyle(
            fontSize: 12,
            fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
            color: Colors.white,
            shadows: [Shadow(color: Colors.black54, blurRadius: 3)],
          ),
          borderSide: const BorderSide(
            color: Color.fromRGBO(255, 255, 255, 0.5),
            width: 2,
          ),
          badgeWidget: _buildBadge(entry.key, entry.value),
          badgePositionPercentageOffset: 1.2,
        ),
      );
    }

    if (othersTotal > 0) {
      final othersPercentage = (othersTotal / total * 100).toStringAsFixed(1);
      final showPercentage = double.parse(othersPercentage) > 5;

      sections.add(
        PieChartSectionData(
          color: colors.last,
          value: othersTotal,
          title: showPercentage ? '$othersPercentage%' : '',
          radius: 24,
          titleStyle: const TextStyle(
            fontSize: 12,
            fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
            color: Colors.white,
            shadows: [Shadow(color: Colors.black54, blurRadius: 3)],
          ),
          borderSide: const BorderSide(
            color: Color.fromRGBO(255, 255, 255, 0.5),
            width: 2,
          ),
          badgeWidget: _buildBadge('Others', othersTotal),
          badgePositionPercentageOffset: 1.2,
        ),
      );
    }

    return sections;
  }

  Widget _buildBadge(String label, double value) {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8, vertical: 4),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Colors.white,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
        boxShadow: const [
          BoxShadow(
            color: Colors.black12,
            blurRadius: 4,
          ),
        ],
      ),
      child: Column(
        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
        children: [
          Text(
            label,
            style: TextStyle(
              fontSize: 10,
              fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
              color: Colors.grey.shade800,
            ),
          ),
          Text(
            _currencyFormat.format(value),
            style: TextStyle(
              fontSize: 12,
              fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
              color: Theme.of(context).primaryColor,
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildSummaryCard(double total, int productCount) {
    return Card(
      elevation: 4,
      child: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
        child: Column(
          children: [
            Text(
              widget.showAllDays ? 'Weekly Summary' : 'Daily Summary',
              style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 20, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 12),
            Row(
              mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
              children: [
                const Text('Total Sales:',
                    style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                Text(
                  _currencyFormat.format(total),
                  style: TextStyle(
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                    fontSize: 16,
                    color: Theme.of(context).primaryColor,
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 8),
            Row(
              mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
              children: [
                const Text('Products Sold:',
                    style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                Text(
                  '$productCount',
                  style: TextStyle(
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                    fontSize: 16,
                    color: Theme.of(context).primaryColor,
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildPieChartSection(
      List<PieChartSectionData> sections, double total) {
    return Column(
      children: [
        SizedBox(
          height: MediaQuery.of(context).size.height * 0.4,
          child: Stack(
            alignment: Alignment.center,
            children: [
              PieChart(
                PieChartData(
                  sections: sections,
                  centerSpaceRadius: 80,
                  sectionsSpace: 0,
                  startDegreeOffset: -90,
                  pieTouchData: PieTouchData(
                    enabled: true,
                    touchCallback: (FlTouchEvent event, pieTouchResponse) {
                      setState(() {
                        if (event is FlTapUpEvent && pieTouchResponse != null) {
                          // Handle tap on sections if needed
                        }
                      });
                    },
                  ),
                ),
                duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 500),
                curve: Curves.easeInOut,
              ),
              Column(
                mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                children: [
                  Text(
                    'TOTAL SALES',
                    style: TextStyle(
                      fontSize: 14,
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                      color: Colors.grey.shade600,
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 4),
                  Text(
                    _currencyFormat.format(total),
                    style: TextStyle(
                      fontSize: 24,
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                      color: Theme.of(context).primaryColor,
                    ),
                  ),
                  if (widget.showAllDays) ...[
                    const SizedBox(height: 4),
                    Text(
                      '${sections.length} ${sections.length == 1 ? 'Category' : 'Categories'}',
                      style: TextStyle(
                        fontSize: 12,
                        color: Colors.grey.shade600,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ],
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 8),
        _buildLegend(sections),
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _buildLegend(List<PieChartSectionData> sections) {
    return Wrap(
      alignment: WrapAlignment.center,
      spacing: 12,
      runSpacing: 8,
      children: sections.map((section) {
        return Row(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          children: [
            Container(
              width: 16,
              height: 16,
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: section.color,
                shape: BoxShape.circle,
                border: Border.all(
                  color: Colors.white,
                  width: 2,
                ),
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(width: 6),
            Text(
              section.title.isNotEmpty
                  ? section.title.split('\n').first.replaceAll('%', '')
                  : 'Others',
              style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 12),
            ),
          ],
        );
      }).toList(),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildForecastSection() {
    return Column(
      children: [
        Text(
          'Sales Forecast (Next 7 Days)',
          style: TextStyle(fontSize: 18, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 8),
        SizedBox(
          height: 250,
          child: SfCartesianChart(
            primaryXAxis: CategoryAxis(),
            primaryYAxis: NumericAxis(
              title: AxisTitle(text: 'Sales Amount (₱)'),
            ),
            series: <CartesianSeries>[
              LineSeries<SalesDataPoint, String>(
                dataSource: _adjustedForecast,
                xValueMapper: (SalesDataPoint sales, _) => sales.day,
                yValueMapper: (SalesDataPoint sales, _) => sales.amount,
                dataLabelSettings: DataLabelSettings(
                  isVisible: true,
                  labelAlignment: ChartDataLabelAlignment.top,
                  textStyle: const TextStyle(fontSize: 10),
                  builder: (data, _, series, a, __) {
                    return Text(
                      _currencyFormat.format(data.amount),
                      style: TextStyle(
                        color: _getForecastTextColor(data),
                        fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                      ),
                    );
                  },
                ),
                name: 'Forecasted Sales',
                color: Theme.of(context).primaryColor,
              )
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  Color _getForecastTextColor(SalesDataPoint data) {
    final baseValue =
        _forecastData.firstWhere((point) => point.day == data.day).amount;
    final difference = data.amount - baseValue;

    if (difference > baseValue * 0.05) return Colors.green;
    if (difference < -baseValue * 0.05) return Colors.red;
    return Colors.grey.shade700;
  }

  void _showForecastExplanation(BuildContext context) {
    showDialog(
      context: context,
      builder: (context) => AlertDialog(
        title: const Text('About Sales Forecasting'),
        content: Column(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: const [
            Text('Our forecast is based on:'),
            SizedBox(height: 8),
            Text('• Last week\'s sales patterns',
                style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
            Text('• 10% projected growth',
                style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
            Text('• Your adjustments for external factors',
                style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
            SizedBox(height: 16),
            Text(
                'Toggle factors to see how different conditions might affect your sales.'),
          ],
        ),
        actions: [
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context),
            child: const Text('Got it!'),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final salesData = Provider.of<SalesData>(context);
    List<Map<String, dynamic>> productsToShow = [];
    double totalAmount = 0;

    if (widget.showAllDays) {
      for (var dayProducts in salesData.currentWeekData.values) {
        productsToShow.addAll(dayProducts);
      }
      totalAmount = productsToShow.fold(0.0, (runningTotal, product) {
        return runningTotal + (product['price'] as num).toDouble();
      });
    } else {
      final dayIndex = widget.date.weekday % 7;
      productsToShow = salesData.currentWeekData[dayIndex] ?? [];
      totalAmount = salesData.getDailyTotal(dayIndex);
    }

    if (salesData.isLoading) {
      return const Scaffold(
        body: Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator()),
      );
    }

    List<PieChartSectionData> pieChartSections =
        _buildPieChartData(productsToShow, totalAmount);

    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: Text(widget.showAllDays
            ? 'Weekly Products Report'
            : 'Daily Report - ${DateFormat('EEE, MMM d, y').format(widget.date)}'),
        actions: [
          IconButton(
            icon: const Icon(Icons.help_outline),
            onPressed: () => _showForecastExplanation(context),
            tooltip: 'How forecasting works',
          ),
          IconButton(
            icon: Icon(_showForecast ? Icons.insert_chart : Icons.trending_up),
            onPressed: () {
              setState(() {
                _showForecast = !_showForecast;
              });
            },
            tooltip: _showForecast ? 'Show Current Data' : 'Show Forecast',
          ),
          IconButton(
            icon: const Icon(Icons.refresh),
            onPressed: () {
              setState(() {
                _forecastData = _generateForecastData(widget.salesData);
                _adjustedForecast = _applyExternalFactors(_forecastData);
              });
            },
            tooltip: 'Refresh Forecast',
          ),
        ],
      ),
      body: SingleChildScrollView(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            _buildSummaryCard(totalAmount, productsToShow.length),
            const SizedBox(height: 20),
            if (_showForecast) ...[
              _buildExternalFactorsPanel(),
              _buildForecastSection(),
              const SizedBox(height: 20),
            ] else ...[
              _buildPieChartSection(pieChartSections, totalAmount),
              const SizedBox(height: 20),
            ],
            Text(
              widget.showAllDays
                  ? 'All Products Sold This Week:'
                  : 'Products Sold:',
              style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 18, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 8),
            ProductListWidget(
              products: productsToShow,
              groupByDay: widget.showAllDays,
              dateFormat: DateFormat('EEEE, MMM d'),
              timeFormat: DateFormat('h:mm a'),
              currencyFormat: _currencyFormat,
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class ProductListWidget extends StatelessWidget {
  final List<Map<String, dynamic>> products;
  final bool groupByDay;
  final DateFormat dateFormat;
  final DateFormat timeFormat;
  final NumberFormat currencyFormat;

  const ProductListWidget({
    super.key,
    required this.products,
    this.groupByDay = false,
    required this.dateFormat,
    required this.timeFormat,
    required this.currencyFormat,
  });

  DateTime _parseFirestoreDate(dynamic date) {
    try {
      if (date == null) return DateTime.now();
      if (date is Timestamp) return date.toDate();
      if (date is DateTime) return date;
      if (date is String) return DateTime.parse(date);
      return DateTime.now();
    } catch (e) {
      return DateTime.now();
    }
  }

  Map<String, List<Map<String, dynamic>>> _groupProductsByDay() {
    final Map<String, List<Map<String, dynamic>>> grouped = {};

    for (var product in products) {
      if (product['date'] != null) {
        final date = _parseFirestoreDate(product['date']);
        final day = dateFormat.format(date);

        grouped.putIfAbsent(day, () => []).add(product);
      }
    }

    return grouped;
  }

  Widget _buildProductItem(Map<String, dynamic> product) {
    final productDate = _parseFirestoreDate(product['date']);
    return Card(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 4),
      child: ListTile(
        title: Text(product['name']?.toString() ?? 'Unknown'),
        subtitle: Text(timeFormat.format(productDate)),
        trailing: Text(
          currencyFormat.format(product['price']),
          style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildDayGroup(String day, List<Map<String, dynamic>> dayProducts) {
    final dayTotal = dayProducts.fold(
      0.0,
      (runningTotal, p) => runningTotal + (p['price'] as num).toDouble(),
    );

    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      children: [
        Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 8.0, horizontal: 16.0),
          child: Row(
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
            children: [
              Text(
                day,
                style:
                    const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 16),
              ),
              Text(
                currencyFormat.format(dayTotal),
                style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
        ...dayProducts.map(_buildProductItem),
        const Divider(),
      ],
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    if (products.isEmpty) {
      return const Center(child: Text('No products sold'));
    }

    return groupByDay
        ? Column(
            children: _groupProductsByDay()
                .entries
                .map((entry) => _buildDayGroup(entry.key, entry.value))
                .toList(),
          )
        : Column(
            children: products.map(_buildProductItem).toList(),
          );
  }
}

class SalesDataPoint {
  final String day;
  final double amount;

  SalesDataPoint(this.day, this.amount);
}

//////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////

import 'package:cloud_firestore/cloud_firestore.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:fl_chart/fl_chart.dart';
import 'package:provider/provider.dart';
import 'package:syncfusion_flutter_charts/charts.dart';
import 'package:intl/intl.dart';
import 'sales_data.dart';

class ProductTrend {
  final String name;
  final double currentPrice;
  final double trend; // Daily rate of change
  final List<double> priceHistory;
  final List<DateTime> dateHistory;
  final LinearRegression regressionModel;

  ProductTrend({
    required this.name,
    required this.currentPrice,
    required this.trend,
    required this.priceHistory,
    required this.dateHistory,
    required this.regressionModel,
  });

  double get projectedNextPrice => regressionModel.predict(dateHistory.last
      .add(const Duration(days: 1))
      .difference(dateHistory.first)
      .inDays
      .toDouble());

  double get projectedPriceInOneWeek => regressionModel.predict(dateHistory.last
      .add(const Duration(days: 7))
      .difference(dateHistory.first)
      .inDays
      .toDouble());
}

class TrendAnalysisService {
  static final TrendAnalysisService _instance =
      TrendAnalysisService._internal();
  factory TrendAnalysisService() => _instance;
  TrendAnalysisService._internal();
  final Map<String, ProductTrend> _productTrends = {};
  DateTime? _lastAnalysisDate;
  final FirebaseFirestore _firestore = FirebaseFirestore.instance;
  Future<void> loadHistoricalTrends() async {
    try {
      final snapshot = await _firestore.collection('product_trends').get();
      _productTrends.clear();

      for (var doc in snapshot.docs) {
        final data = doc.data();
        final name = doc.id;

        // Safer price history conversion
        final priceHistory = List<double>.from(data['priceHistory'] ?? []);

        // Safer date history conversion
        final dateHistory = (data['dateHistory'] as List?)?.map((item) {
              if (item is Timestamp) {
                return item.toDate();
              } else if (item is DateTime) {
                return item;
              }
              return DateTime.now(); // fallback
            }).toList() ??
            [];

        if (priceHistory.isNotEmpty && dateHistory.isNotEmpty) {
          final regression =
              _calculateLinearRegression(priceHistory, dateHistory);

          _productTrends[name] = ProductTrend(
            name: name,
            currentPrice: priceHistory.last,
            trend: regression.slope,
            priceHistory: priceHistory,
            dateHistory: dateHistory,
            regressionModel: regression,
          );
        }
      }
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('Error loading historical trends: $e');
    }
  }

  Future<void> analyzeSalesData(SalesData salesData) async {
    await loadHistoricalTrends(); // Load existing trends first

    final productSalesMap = <String, List<Map<String, dynamic>>>{};

    // Process all available historical data, not just current week
    for (var weekData in salesData.monthlyProducts.values) {
      for (var dayProducts in weekData.values) {
        for (var product in dayProducts) {
          final name = product['name'] ?? 'Unknown';
          productSalesMap.putIfAbsent(name, () => []).add(product);
        }
      }
    }

    // Process current week data
    for (var dayProducts in salesData.currentWeekData.values) {
      for (var product in dayProducts) {
        final name = product['name'] ?? 'Unknown';
        productSalesMap.putIfAbsent(name, () => []).add(product);
      }
    }

    // Update trends for each product
    for (var entry in productSalesMap.entries) {
      final existingTrend = _productTrends[entry.key];
      final newTrend = _analyzeProductTrend(entry.key, entry.value);

      // Merge with existing trend data if available
      if (existingTrend != null) {
        final mergedPrices = [
          ...existingTrend.priceHistory,
          ...newTrend.priceHistory
        ];
        final mergedDates = [
          ...existingTrend.dateHistory,
          ...newTrend.dateHistory
        ];
        final mergedRegression =
            _calculateLinearRegression(mergedPrices, mergedDates);

        _productTrends[entry.key] = ProductTrend(
          name: entry.key,
          currentPrice: newTrend.currentPrice,
          trend: mergedRegression.slope,
          priceHistory: mergedPrices,
          dateHistory: mergedDates,
          regressionModel: mergedRegression,
        );
      } else {
        _productTrends[entry.key] = newTrend;
      }
    }

    await saveCurrentTrends();
    _lastAnalysisDate = DateTime.now();
  }

  ProductTrend _analyzeProductTrend(
      String name, List<Map<String, dynamic>> sales) {
    // Sort sales by date to ensure chronological order
    sales
        .sort((a, b) => _parseDate(a['date']).compareTo(_parseDate(b['date'])));

    final prices = sales.map((s) => (s['price'] as num).toDouble()).toList();
    final dates = sales.map((s) => _parseDate(s['date'])).toList();

    // Debug: Print price history
    debugPrint('Price history for $name: $prices');
    debugPrint('Date history for $name: $dates');

    final regression = _calculateLinearRegression(prices, dates);

    return ProductTrend(
      name: name,
      currentPrice: prices.last,
      trend: regression.slope,
      priceHistory: prices,
      dateHistory: dates,
      regressionModel: regression,
    );
  }

  Future<void> saveCurrentTrends() async {
    try {
      final batch = _firestore.batch();

      for (var entry in _productTrends.entries) {
        final docRef = _firestore.collection('product_trends').doc(entry.key);
        batch.set(docRef, {
          'priceHistory': entry.value.priceHistory,
          'dateHistory': entry.value.dateHistory
              .map((d) => Timestamp.fromDate(d))
              .toList(),
          'lastUpdated': FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
        });
      }

      await batch.commit();
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('Error saving trends: $e');
    }
  }

  LinearRegression _calculateLinearRegression(
      List<double> prices, List<DateTime> dates) {
    if (prices.isEmpty || dates.isEmpty || prices.length != dates.length) {
      return LinearRegression(slope: 0, intercept: 0);
    }

    final firstDate = dates.first;
    final xValues =
        dates.map((d) => d.difference(firstDate).inDays.toDouble()).toList();

    double sumX = 0, sumY = 0, sumXY = 0, sumXX = 0;
    final n = prices.length;

    for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
      sumX += xValues[i];
      sumY += prices[i];
      sumXY += xValues[i] * prices[i];
      sumXX += xValues[i] * xValues[i];
    }

    final slope = (n * sumXY - sumX * sumY) / (n * sumXX - sumX * sumX);
    final intercept = (sumY - slope * sumX) / n;

    return LinearRegression(slope: slope, intercept: intercept);
  }

  DateTime _parseDate(dynamic date) {
    if (date is Timestamp) return date.toDate();
    if (date is DateTime) return date;
    if (date is String) return DateTime.parse(date);
    return DateTime.now();
  }

  List<ProductTrend> getProductTrends() => _productTrends.values.toList();

  List<ProductTrend> getTopPerformingProducts() =>
      _productTrends.values.where((trend) => trend.trend > 0).toList()
        ..sort((a, b) => b.trend.compareTo(a.trend));

  List<ProductTrend> getBottomPerformingProducts() =>
      _productTrends.values.where((trend) => trend.trend < 0).toList()
        ..sort((a, b) => a.trend.compareTo(b.trend));

  DateTime? get lastAnalysisDate => _lastAnalysisDate;
}

class DailyReportScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  final DateTime date;
  final SalesData salesData;
  final bool showAllDays;

  const DailyReportScreen({
    super.key,
    required this.date,
    required this.salesData,
    this.showAllDays = false,
  });

  @override
  State<DailyReportScreen> createState() => _DailyReportScreenState();
}

final NumberFormat _currencyFormat = NumberFormat.currency(
  locale: 'en_PH',
  symbol: '₱',
  decimalDigits: 2,
);

class _DailyReportScreenState extends State<DailyReportScreen> {
  final TrendAnalysisService _trendAnalysis = TrendAnalysisService();
  bool _isAnalyzing = false;
  bool _showForecast = false;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _loadAnalysis();
  }

  Future<void> _loadAnalysis() async {
    setState(() => _isAnalyzing = true);
    try {
      // Load historical trends first
      await _trendAnalysis.loadHistoricalTrends();
      // Then analyze current data
      await _trendAnalysis.analyzeSalesData(widget.salesData);
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint('Error loading analysis: $e');
    }
    setState(() => _isAnalyzing = false);
  }

  Future<void> _refreshAnalysis() async {
    setState(() => _isAnalyzing = true);
    await _trendAnalysis.analyzeSalesData(widget.salesData);
    setState(() => _isAnalyzing = false);
  }

  List<PieChartSectionData> _buildPieChartData(
      List<Map<String, dynamic>> products, double total) {
    if (products.isEmpty || total <= 0) {
      return [
        PieChartSectionData(
          color: Colors.grey[300],
          value: 1,
          title: 'No Data',
          radius: 20,
          titleStyle: const TextStyle(
            fontSize: 14,
            fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
            color: Colors.black54,
          ),
        )
      ];
    }

    final productTotals = <String, double>{};
    for (var product in products) {
      final name = product['name'] ?? 'Unknown';
      final price = (product['price'] as num).toDouble();
      productTotals.update(name, (value) => value + price,
          ifAbsent: () => price);
    }

    final sortedEntries = productTotals.entries.toList()
      ..sort((a, b) => b.value.compareTo(a.value));

    final topProducts = sortedEntries.take(5).toList();
    final othersTotal = sortedEntries
        .skip(5)
        .fold(0.0, (runningTotal, entry) => runningTotal + entry.value);

    final colors = [
      Colors.indigo.shade400,
      Colors.teal.shade400,
      Colors.amber.shade600,
      Colors.pink.shade400,
      Colors.deepPurple.shade400,
      Colors.grey.shade500,
    ];

    final sections = <PieChartSectionData>[];

    for (int i = 0; i < topProducts.length; i++) {
      final entry = topProducts[i];
      final percentage = (entry.value / total * 100).toStringAsFixed(1);

      sections.add(
        PieChartSectionData(
          color: colors[i],
          value: entry.value,
          title: double.parse(percentage) > 5 ? '$percentage%' : '',
          radius: 24,
          titleStyle: const TextStyle(
            fontSize: 12,
            fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
            color: Colors.white,
            shadows: [Shadow(color: Colors.black54, blurRadius: 3)],
          ),
          borderSide: const BorderSide(
            color: Color.fromRGBO(255, 255, 255, 0.5),
            width: 2,
          ),
          badgeWidget: _buildBadge(entry.key, entry.value),
          badgePositionPercentageOffset: 1.2,
        ),
      );
    }

    if (othersTotal > 0) {
      final othersPercentage = (othersTotal / total * 100).toStringAsFixed(1);
      final showPercentage = double.parse(othersPercentage) > 5;

      sections.add(
        PieChartSectionData(
          color: colors.last,
          value: othersTotal,
          title: showPercentage ? '$othersPercentage%' : '',
          radius: 24,
          titleStyle: const TextStyle(
            fontSize: 12,
            fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
            color: Colors.white,
            shadows: [Shadow(color: Colors.black54, blurRadius: 3)],
          ),
          borderSide: const BorderSide(
            color: Color.fromRGBO(255, 255, 255, 0.5),
            width: 2,
          ),
          badgeWidget: _buildBadge('Others', othersTotal),
          badgePositionPercentageOffset: 1.2,
        ),
      );
    }

    return sections;
  }

  Widget _buildBadge(String label, double value) {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8, vertical: 4),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: Colors.white,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
        boxShadow: const [
          BoxShadow(
            color: Colors.black12,
            blurRadius: 4,
          ),
        ],
      ),
      child: Column(
        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
        children: [
          Text(
            label,
            style: TextStyle(
              fontSize: 10,
              fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
              color: Colors.grey.shade800,
            ),
          ),
          Text(
            _currencyFormat.format(value),
            style: TextStyle(
              fontSize: 12,
              fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
              color: Theme.of(context).primaryColor,
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildSummaryCard(double total, int productCount) {
    return Card(
      elevation: 4,
      child: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
        child: Column(
          children: [
            Text(
              widget.showAllDays ? 'Weekly Summary' : 'Daily Summary',
              style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 20, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 12),
            Row(
              mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
              children: [
                const Text('Total Sales:',
                    style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                Text(
                  _currencyFormat.format(total),
                  style: TextStyle(
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                    fontSize: 16,
                    color: Theme.of(context).primaryColor,
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 8),
            Row(
              mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
              children: [
                const Text('Products Sold:',
                    style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                Text(
                  '$productCount',
                  style: TextStyle(
                    fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                    fontSize: 16,
                    color: Theme.of(context).primaryColor,
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildPieChartSection(
      List<PieChartSectionData> sections, double total) {
    return Column(
      children: [
        SizedBox(
          height: MediaQuery.of(context).size.height * 0.4,
          child: Stack(
            alignment: Alignment.center,
            children: [
              PieChart(
                PieChartData(
                  sections: sections,
                  centerSpaceRadius: 80,
                  sectionsSpace: 0,
                  startDegreeOffset: -90,
                  pieTouchData: PieTouchData(
                    enabled: true,
                    touchCallback: (FlTouchEvent event, pieTouchResponse) {
                      setState(() {
                        if (event is FlTapUpEvent && pieTouchResponse != null) {
                          // Handle tap on sections if needed
                        }
                      });
                    },
                  ),
                ),
                duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 500),
                curve: Curves.easeInOut,
              ),
              Column(
                mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                children: [
                  Text(
                    'TOTAL SALES',
                    style: TextStyle(
                      fontSize: 14,
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                      color: Colors.grey.shade600,
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 4),
                  Text(
                    _currencyFormat.format(total),
                    style: TextStyle(
                      fontSize: 24,
                      fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                      color: Theme.of(context).primaryColor,
                    ),
                  ),
                  if (widget.showAllDays) ...[
                    const SizedBox(height: 4),
                    Text(
                      '${sections.length} ${sections.length == 1 ? 'Category' : 'Categories'}',
                      style: TextStyle(
                        fontSize: 12,
                        color: Colors.grey.shade600,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ],
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 8),
        _buildLegend(sections),
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _buildLegend(List<PieChartSectionData> sections) {
    return Wrap(
      alignment: WrapAlignment.center,
      spacing: 12,
      runSpacing: 8,
      children: sections.map((section) {
        return Row(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          children: [
            Container(
              width: 16,
              height: 16,
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: section.color,
                shape: BoxShape.circle,
                border: Border.all(
                  color: Colors.white,
                  width: 2,
                ),
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(width: 6),
            Text(
              section.title.isNotEmpty
                  ? section.title.split('\n').first.replaceAll('%', '')
                  : 'Others',
              style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 12),
            ),
          ],
        );
      }).toList(),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildForecastSection() {
    final productTrends = _trendAnalysis.getProductTrends()
      ..sort((a, b) => b.currentPrice.compareTo(a.currentPrice));

    return Column(
      children: [
        const Text(
          'Product Expenditure Trend Analysis',
          style: TextStyle(fontSize: 18, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
        ),
        if (_trendAnalysis.lastAnalysisDate != null)
          Text(
            'Last analyzed: ${DateFormat('MMM d, y - h:mm a').format(_trendAnalysis.lastAnalysisDate!)}',
            style: TextStyle(color: Colors.grey[600], fontSize: 12),
          ),
        const SizedBox(height: 16),
        SizedBox(
          height: 300,
          child: SfCartesianChart(
            primaryXAxis:
                NumericAxis(title: AxisTitle(text: 'Current Price (₱)')),
            primaryYAxis: NumericAxis(title: AxisTitle(text: 'Daily Trend')),
            series: <CartesianSeries>[
              ScatterSeries<ProductTrend, double>(
                dataSource: productTrends,
                xValueMapper: (trend, _) => trend.currentPrice,
                yValueMapper: (trend, _) => trend.trend,
                dataLabelSettings: const DataLabelSettings(isVisible: true),
                markerSettings: const MarkerSettings(isVisible: true),
                name: 'Products',
                color: Colors.blue,
              ),
            ],
            tooltipBehavior: TooltipBehavior(
              enable: true,
              builder: (data, point, series, pointIndex, seriesIndex) {
                final trend = productTrends[pointIndex];
                return Container(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(8),
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    color: Colors.white,
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(4),
                  ),
                  child: Column(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                    children: [
                      Text(trend.name,
                          style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
                      const SizedBox(height: 4),
                      Text(
                          'Current: ${_currencyFormat.format(trend.currentPrice)}'),
                      Text('Trend: ${trend.trend.toStringAsFixed(2)}/day'),
                      Text(
                          'Next day: ${_currencyFormat.format(trend.projectedNextPrice)}'),
                      Text(
                          'Next week: ${_currencyFormat.format(trend.projectedPriceInOneWeek)}'),
                    ],
                  ),
                );
              },
            ),
          ),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 20),
        _buildTrendProductsTable(),
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _buildTrendProductsTable() {
    final topPerformers = _trendAnalysis.getTopPerformingProducts();
    final decliningProducts = _trendAnalysis.getBottomPerformingProducts();

    // Debug view to check what's being loaded
    if (topPerformers.isEmpty && decliningProducts.isEmpty) {
      return Column(
        children: [
          Text('No trend data available', style: TextStyle(color: Colors.grey)),
          ElevatedButton(
            onPressed: _refreshAnalysis,
            child: Text('Retry Analysis'),
          ),
          // Show raw data for debugging
          ExpansionTile(
            title: Text('Debug: All Product Trends'),
            children: _trendAnalysis
                .getProductTrends()
                .map((trend) => ListTile(
                      title: Text(trend.name),
                      subtitle:
                          Text('Trend: ${trend.trend.toStringAsFixed(2)}'),
                      trailing:
                          Text('Data points: ${trend.priceHistory.length}'),
                    ))
                .toList(),
          ),
        ],
      );
    }

    return DefaultTabController(
      length: 2,
      child: Column(
        children: [
          const TabBar(
            tabs: [
              Tab(text: 'Top Performers'),
              Tab(text: 'Declining Products'),
            ],
          ),
          SizedBox(
            height: 300,
            child: TabBarView(
              children: [
                _buildProductTrendList(topPerformers),
                _buildProductTrendList(decliningProducts),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildProductTrendList(List<ProductTrend> trends) {
    return ListView.builder(
      itemCount: trends.length,
      itemBuilder: (context, index) {
        final trend = trends[index];
        return ListTile(
          title: Text(trend.name),
          subtitle: Text('Trend: ${trend.trend.toStringAsFixed(2)}/day'),
          trailing: Column(
            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.end,
            children: [
              Text(_currencyFormat.format(trend.currentPrice)),
              Text(
                _currencyFormat.format(trend.projectedNextPrice),
                style: TextStyle(
                  color: trend.trend >= 0 ? Colors.green : Colors.red,
                  fontWeight: FontWeight.bold,
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        );
      },
    );
  }

  void _showForecastExplanation(BuildContext context) {
    showDialog(
      context: context,
      builder: (context) => AlertDialog(
        title: const Text('About Product Expenditure Analysis'),
        content: Column(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: const [
            Text('This analysis shows:'),
            SizedBox(height: 8),
            Text('• Average expenditure per product',
                style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
            Text('• Trend slope (positive = increasing, negative = decreasing)',
                style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
            Text('• Linear regression model predicting future expenditure',
                style: TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold)),
            SizedBox(height: 16),
            Text(
                'Products in the top-right quadrant are your high-value, growing products.'),
          ],
        ),
        actions: [
          TextButton(
            onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context),
            child: const Text('Got it!'),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final salesData = Provider.of<SalesData>(context);
    List<Map<String, dynamic>> productsToShow = [];
    double totalAmount = 0;

    if (widget.showAllDays) {
      for (var dayProducts in salesData.currentWeekData.values) {
        productsToShow.addAll(dayProducts);
      }
      totalAmount = productsToShow.fold(0.0, (runningTotal, product) {
        return runningTotal + (product['price'] as num).toDouble();
      });
    } else {
      final dayIndex = widget.date.weekday % 7;
      productsToShow = salesData.currentWeekData[dayIndex] ?? [];
      totalAmount = salesData.getDailyTotal(dayIndex);
    }

    if (salesData.isLoading || _isAnalyzing) {
      return const Scaffold(
        body: Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator()),
      );
    }

    List<PieChartSectionData> pieChartSections =
        _buildPieChartData(productsToShow, totalAmount);

    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: Text(widget.showAllDays
            ? 'Weekly Products Report'
            : 'Daily Report - ${DateFormat('EEE, MMM d, y').format(widget.date)}'),
        actions: [
          IconButton(
            icon: const Icon(Icons.help_outline),
            onPressed: () => _showForecastExplanation(context),
            tooltip: 'How analysis works',
          ),
          IconButton(
            icon: Icon(_showForecast ? Icons.insert_chart : Icons.trending_up),
            onPressed: () => setState(() => _showForecast = !_showForecast),
            tooltip:
                _showForecast ? 'Show Current Data' : 'Show Trend Analysis',
          ),
          IconButton(
            icon: const Icon(Icons.refresh),
            onPressed: _refreshAnalysis,
            tooltip: 'Refresh Analysis',
          ),
        ],
      ),
      body: SingleChildScrollView(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16.0),
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            _buildSummaryCard(totalAmount, productsToShow.length),
            const SizedBox(height: 20),
            if (_showForecast) ...[
              _buildForecastSection(),
              const SizedBox(height: 20),
            ] else ...[
              _buildPieChartSection(pieChartSections, totalAmount),
              const SizedBox(height: 20),
            ],
            Text(
              widget.showAllDays
                  ? 'All Products Sold This Week:'
                  : 'Products Sold:',
              style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 18, fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 8),
            ProductListWidget(
              products: productsToShow,
              groupByDay: widget.showAllDays,
              dateFormat: DateFormat('EEEE, MMM d'),
              timeFormat: DateFormat('h:mm a'),
              currencyFormat: _currencyFormat,
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class LinearRegression {
  final double slope;
  final double intercept;

  LinearRegression({required this.slope, required this.intercept});

  double predict(double x) => slope * x + intercept;
}

class ProductListWidget extends StatelessWidget {
  final List<Map<String, dynamic>> products;
  final bool groupByDay;
  final DateFormat dateFormat;
  final DateFormat timeFormat;
  final NumberFormat currencyFormat;

  const ProductListWidget({
    super.key,
    required this.products,
    this.groupByDay = false,
    required this.dateFormat,
    required this.timeFormat,
    required this.currencyFormat,
  });

  DateTime _parseFirestoreDate(dynamic date) {
    try {
      if (date == null) return DateTime.now();
      if (date is Timestamp) return date.toDate();
      if (date is DateTime) return date;
      if (date is String) return DateTime.parse(date);
      return DateTime.now();
    } catch (e) {
      return DateTime.now();
    }
  }

  Map<String, List<Map<String, dynamic>>> _groupProductsByDay() {
    final Map<String, List<Map<String, dynamic>>> grouped = {};

    for (var product in products) {
      if (product['date'] != null) {
        final date = _parseFirestoreDate(product['date']);
        final day = dateFormat.format(date);

        grouped.putIfAbsent(day, () => []).add(product);
      }
    }

    return grouped;
  }

  Widget _buildProductItem(Map<String, dynamic> product) {
    final productDate = _parseFirestoreDate(product['date']);
    return Card(
      margin: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 4),
      child: ListTile(
        title: Text(product['name']?.toString() ?? 'Unknown'),
        subtitle: Text(timeFormat.format(productDate)),
        trailing: Text(
          currencyFormat.format(product['price']),
          style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildDayGroup(String day, List<Map<String, dynamic>> dayProducts) {
    final dayTotal = dayProducts.fold(
      0.0,
      (runningTotal, p) => runningTotal + (p['price'] as num).toDouble(),
    );

    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      children: [
        Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 8.0, horizontal: 16.0),
          child: Row(
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
            children: [
              Text(
                day,
                style:
                    const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold, fontSize: 16),
              ),
              Text(
                currencyFormat.format(dayTotal),
                style: const TextStyle(fontWeight: FontWeight.bold),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
        ...dayProducts.map(_buildProductItem),
        const Divider(),
      ],
    );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    if (products.isEmpty) {
      return const Center(child: Text('No products sold'));
    }

    return groupByDay
        ? Column(
            children: _groupProductsByDay()
                .entries
                .map((entry) => _buildDayGroup(entry.key, entry.value))
                .toList(),
          )
        : Column(
            children: products.map(_buildProductItem).toList(),
          );
  }
}
